Purpose


Verify that login is rejected when an invalid username is paired with a valid password.

Preconditions


  • ${LIMAS_BASE_URL} is reachable.

  • Valid password from SUPER_USER_ADMIN_USER is configured.

    • Expected Result


    Authentication is rejected, error feedback is displayed, and the Keycloak login page remains active.

    Expected Result

    Error feedback is visible and user stays on Keycloak login form.

    Test: LIMAS-AUTH-031

    Module: Authentication

    Spec: authentication/LIMAS-AUTH-031-empty-username-valid-password.spec.ts

    Purpose


    Verify that login is rejected when the username field is empty but a valid password is supplied.

    Preconditions


  • ${LIMAS_BASE_URL} is reachable.

  • Valid password from SUPER_USER_ADMIN_USER is configured.

    • Expected Result


    Authentication is rejected due to missing username, feedback is displayed, and the Keycloak login page remains active.

    Expected Result

    Login is rejected with error feedback.

    Test Steps

    1. Login page loaded
    2. Sign In clicked
    3. Keycloak login form visible

    Test Plan

    Purpose

    Login: Empty username + valid password → Rejection

    Application URL is reachable.

    Test Steps

    1. Open the application URL.
    2. Click "Sign In".
    3. Leave username empty, enter valid password.
    4. Submit.

    Expected Final Result

    Login is rejected with error feedback.

    Test: LIMAS-AUTH-041

    Module: Authentication
    Access Profile: SUPER_USER_ADMIN_USER
    Spec: authentication/LIMAS-AUTH-041-session-state-cleared-after-signout.spec.ts
    Plan: LIMAS-AUTH-041-session-state-cleared-after-signout.md

    Purpose


    Verify that after sign-out, stored authenticated state is no longer usable and authentication-dependent content is not visible.

    Preconditions


  • ${LIMAS_BASE_URL} is reachable.

  • Valid credentials for SUPER_USER_ADMIN_USER are configured.

    • Expected Result


    After sign-out and storage clearance, no authenticated content is accessible without re-authentication.

    Expected Result

    Session unavailable; login page displayed.
